# Nuqta × Da’ira / نقطة × دائرة

مشروع Next.js كامل للعبة محلية عربية بنمط Cosmic Neon Arena، مبني بـ React وTypeScript وTailwind CSS وFramer Motion وLucide React، مع منطق لعب مفصول، تخزين آمن في LocalStorage، مؤثرات صوتية مولدة عبر Web Audio API، ودعم RTL كامل.

## Project Structure And Decisions

- App Router في Next.js مع واجهة عميل واحدة تدير اللعب والإعدادات والإحصاءات بدون Backend.
- منطق اللعبة النقي في `lib/gameLogic.ts`، والتحقق من التخزين في `lib/storage.ts` لتجنب انهيار التطبيق عند تلف LocalStorage.
- مكونات قابلة لإعادة الاستخدام للوحة، الخلايا، البطاقات، الشيتات، الحوار التأكيدي، الخلفية النيونية، والرموز المتحركة.
- الاختبارات تغطي المنطق والتخزين وتدفقات المتصفح الأساسية على الهاتف وسطح المكتب.

## Final File Tree

```text
.
├── app/
│   ├── globals.css
│   ├── icon.svg
│   ├── layout.tsx
│   ├── not-found.tsx
│   └── page.tsx
├── components/
│   ├── ConfirmDialog.tsx
│   ├── GameApp.tsx
│   ├── GameBoard.tsx
│   ├── GameCell.tsx
│   ├── GameHeader.tsx
│   ├── IconButton.tsx
│   ├── NeonBackground.tsx
│   ├── PlayerCard.tsx
│   ├── PlayerSymbol.tsx
│   ├── ResultModal.tsx
│   ├── SettingsSheet.tsx
│   ├── Sheet.tsx
│   ├── StartScreen.tsx
│   └── StatisticsSheet.tsx
├── hooks/
│   ├── useGame.ts
│   ├── useHapticFeedback.ts
│   ├── useLocalStorage.ts
│   └── useSound.ts
├── lib/
│   ├── constants.ts
│   ├── gameLogic.ts
│   ├── storage.ts
│   └── utils.ts
├── tests/
│   ├── e2e/game.spec.ts
│   ├── gameLogic.test.ts
│   └── storage.test.ts
├── types/game.ts
├── eslint.config.mjs
├── next-env.d.ts
├── next.config.ts
├── package-lock.json
├── package.json
├── playwright.config.ts
├── postcss.config.mjs
├── README.md
├── tsconfig.json
└── vitest.config.ts
```

## Installation, Setup, And Run Commands

```bash
npm install
npm run dev

# Production check
npm run build
npm run start

# Validation
npm run lint
npm run typecheck
npm test
npm run test:e2e
```

## Full File Contents

### .gitignore

```text
node_modules/
.next/
out/
coverage/
playwright-report/
test-results/
*.tsbuildinfo
.DS_Store
```

### package.json

```json
{
  "name": "nuqta-daira",
  "version": "1.0.0",
  "description": "Arabic local two-player neon tic-tac-toe",
  "scripts": {
    "dev": "next dev --hostname 0.0.0.0",
    "build": "next build",
    "start": "next start --hostname 0.0.0.0",
    "lint": "eslint .",
    "typecheck": "tsc --noEmit",
    "test": "vitest run",
    "test:watch": "vitest",
    "test:e2e": "playwright test"
  },
  "license": "ISC",
  "dependencies": {
    "framer-motion": "^13.2.0",
    "lucide-react": "^1.45.0",
    "next": "^16.3.5",
    "react": "^19.3.0",
    "react-dom": "^19.3.0"
  },
  "devDependencies": {
    "@playwright/test": "^1.63.0",
    "@tailwindcss/postcss": "^4.3.3",
    "@types/node": "^22.20.2",
    "@types/react": "^19.3.0",
    "@types/react-dom": "^19.3.0",
    "eslint": "^9.39.5",
    "eslint-config-next": "^16.3.5",
    "postcss": "^8.5.28",
    "tailwindcss": "^4.3.3",
    "typescript": "^6.0.3",
    "vitest": "^5.0.0"
  },
  "private": true,
  "type": "module",
  "engines": {
    "node": ">=22.0.0"
  }
}
```

### README.md

```md
# نقطة × دائرة

لعبة عربية محلية للاعبين على جهاز واحد، بتصميم Cosmic Neon Arena. لا توجد حسابات أو خدمات خلفية أو تنزيلات خطوط أو ملفات صوت خارجية.

## التشغيل

يتطلب المشروع Node.js 22 أو أحدث وnpm.

```bash
npm ci
npm run dev
```

افتح http://localhost:3000. لبناء وتشغيل نسخة الإنتاج:

```bash
npm run build
npm start
```

الاتصال بالإنترنت مطلوب لتثبيت الحزم فقط. لا تجري اللعبة طلبات إلى خدمات خارجية أثناء التشغيل. لا يتضمن المشروع Service Worker أو تثبيت PWA.

## البنية

- `app/`: صفحة App Router وتخطيط عربي خادمي وCSS والهوية المحلية.
- `components/`: جذر التفاعل وشاشات البداية واللعب والنوافذ والعناصر المشتركة.
- `hooks/`: حالة اللعبة والتخزين والصوت والاهتزاز.
- `lib/`: منطق اللعبة النقي والتحقق من التخزين والثوابت والتنسيق.
- `types/`: أنواع اللوحة واللاعبين والإعدادات والإحصاءات والأحداث.
- `tests/`: اختبارات المنطق والتخزين واختبارات المتصفح.

`GameApp` هو حد التفاعل العميل. يحدّث reducer نقي اللوحة ونتائج المباراة والإحصاءات في انتقال واحد؛ لا تُحتسب النتائج في effects. لا يقرأ العرض الخادمي LocalStorage. تكتمل القراءة قبل تفعيل اللعبة أو كتابة الإعدادات والإحصاءات.

تُرسم الرموز وخطوط الفوز باستخدام SVG. إحداثيات اللوحة الداخلية تبدأ من أعلى اليسار حتى تتطابق الخانات مع خط الفوز، فيما تبقى الواجهة والنصوص RTL. أعمدة تسميات قارئ الشاشة مرقّمة من اليسار. تعرض الإحصاءات أرقامًا إنجليزية ثابتة التنسيق.

## القواعد

- يبدأ × المباراة الأولى. بعد الفوز يبدأ الخاسر؛ بعد التعادل يبدأ اللاعب الذي لم يبدأ الجولة السابقة.
- «جولة جديدة» أثناء اللعب تطلب تأكيدًا، ثم تلغي الجولة دون احتسابها أو تغيير رقمها أو بادئها. بعد اكتمالها تنتقل للجولة التالية.
- «إعادة المباراة» تصفّر نتائج المباراة فقط، وتعيد البادئ إلى × ورقم الجولة إلى 1.
- العودة للرئيسية تنهي المباراة. تُطلب الموافقة إذا كانت الجولة غير مكتملة وبها حركات. تحديث الصفحة ينهي المباراة أيضًا؛ لا تُحفظ اللوحة.
- تُحفظ الإحصاءات بحسب الرمز وليس اسم اللاعب. التعادل يكسر سلسلة الانتصارات. نسبة الفوز هي عدد الانتصارات مقسومًا على جميع الجولات المكتملة، بما فيها التعادلات. عند تساوي أفضل سلسلة تُحفظ السلسلة السابقة.
- تصفير الإحصاءات منفصل عن إعادة الإعدادات والمباراة.

## التخزين والخصائص الاختيارية

المفتاحان هما `nuqta-daira:stats:v1` و`nuqta-daira:settings:v1`. يتحقق التطبيق من JSON والقيم، ويستعيد الافتراضي عند التلف. إذا حُظر التخزين يستمر اللعب في الذاكرة وتظهر رسالة عربية.

تُحفظ الأسماء والإعدادات أثناء التعديل. الأسماء لا تتجاوز 24 حرفًا وتعود للاسم الافتراضي عند ترك الحقل فارغًا. السمات تُطبق فورًا. الصوت والاهتزاز مفعّلان افتراضيًا. ينشأ AudioContext داخل تفاعل المستخدم فقط؛ الاهتزاز اختياري ويعتمد على دعم المتصفح والجهاز.

الإحصاءات مخصصة لجلسة نشطة واحدة؛ ليست مزامنة متعددة التبويبات أو الأجهزة.

## الاختبارات

```bash
npm run typecheck
npm run lint
npm test
npx playwright install chromium
npm run test:e2e
```

يشغّل Playwright خادم التطوير تلقائيًا إن لم يكن يعمل بالفعل. لتشغيل اختبار واحد: `npm run test:e2e -- --grep "draw"`.

## Final Verification

1. شغّل المشروع بالأوامر أعلاه، وابدأ جولة.
2. لتجربة فوز ×، انقر الخانات بالترتيب `0, 3, 1, 4, 2`. لتجربة فوز ○، استخدم `0, 3, 1, 4, 8, 5`. لفحص التعادل استخدم `0, 1, 2, 4, 3, 5, 7, 6, 8`. تبدأ الفهارس من أعلى اليسار وتنتقل إلى اليمين، صفًا بعد صف.
3. أغلق نافذة النتيجة وتأكد من بقاء اللوحة مقفلة، ثم ابدأ الجولة التالية وتحقق من البادئ الجديد.
4. غيّر الأسماء والسمات والصوت ثم أعد تحميل الصفحة؛ افتح الإعدادات وتحقق من بقائها. أكمل جولة وأعد التحميل، ثم افتح الإحصاءات وتحقق من حفظها.
5. جرّب إلغاء تأكيد الحذف ثم الموافقة عليه، وكذلك إعادة المباراة والإعدادات. تحقّق من أن كل إجراء يغيّر البيانات المقصودة فقط.
6. جرّب المقاسات 360×640 و390×844 و768×1024 وسطح المكتب. افحص السمات الثلاث، النص العربي، حواف الأمان وعدم وجود تمرير أفقي. عند قصر الشاشة الشديد أو تكبير النص يُسمح بالتمرير بدل قص المحتوى.
7. استخدم Tab وEnter وSpace وEscape. تُعلن الأدوار والنتائج عبر aria-live، وتحجز النوافذ التركيز وتعيده إلى العنصر السابق. فعّل تقليل الحركة من نظام التشغيل وتحقق من إيقاف الحركة المستمرة.
8. اختبر الصوت بعد نقرة فعلية على الهاتف، والاهتزاز على جهاز يدعم `navigator.vibrate`؛ عدم توفرهما لا يمنع اللعب.
```

import type { Metadata, Viewport } from "next";
import type { ReactNode } from "react";
import "@/app/globals.css";

export const metadata: Metadata = {
  title: "نقطة × دائرة | ساحة النيون",
  description: "تحدَّ صديقك في ساحة النيون. لعبة نقطة × دائرة للاعبين على جهاز واحد، بأجواء كونية وتجربة عربية كاملة.",
  applicationName: "نقطة × دائرة",
};
export const viewport: Viewport = { width: "device-width", initialScale: 1, viewportFit: "cover", themeColor: "#070812" };
export default function RootLayout({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {

}

### app/globals.css

```css
@import "tailwindcss";

:root { color-scheme: dark; font-family: "Cairo", "Tajawal", system-ui, sans-serif; background: #070812; color: #f4f5ff; }
* { box-sizing: border-box; }
body { margin: 0; }
button, input { font: inherit; }
button { cursor: pointer; -webkit-tap-highlight-color: transparent; touch-action: manipulation; }
button:disabled { opacity: .45; cursor: default; }
button, a, input { outline-offset: 5px; }
:focus-visible { outline: 2px solid var(--x, #35dfff); }
button { transition: background .2s, border-color .2s, transform .18s, box-shadow .2s; }
button:active:not(:disabled) { transform: scale(.97); }
button svg { flex-shrink: 0; }
h1, h2, h3, p { margin: 0; }
fieldset { min-width: 0; padding: 0; border: 0; margin: 0; }
.app-shell { --bg: #070812; --surface: #11152d; --x: #35dfff; --o: #ff65ca; --accent: #8054ff; --text: #f4f5ff; --muted: #a5aac5; --line: #ffffff12; --panel: #101126; position: relative; isolation: isolate; min-height: 100svh; color: var(--text); background: var(--bg); overflow-x: clip; }
.app-shell[data-theme="sunset"] { --bg: #100a19; --surface: #29172e; --x: #ffb773; --o: #f58aff; --accent: #ca69d6; --muted: #c4acc8; --panel: #211327; }
.app-shell[data-theme="ice"] { --bg: #050e1b; --surface: #10243b; --x: #75eeff; --o: #b5b9ff; --accent: #4e8ffc; --muted: #a6bdd2; --panel: #0c1a2e; }
.text-x, .symbol-x { color: var(--x); }
.text-o, .symbol-o { color: var(--o); }
.neon-background { position: fixed; inset: 0; z-index: -1; pointer-events: none; overflow: hidden; background: radial-gradient(ellipse at 50% 10%, color-mix(in srgb, var(--accent) 9%, transparent), transparent 60%); }
.nebula { position: absolute; width: min(60vw, 650px); aspect-ratio: 1; border-radius: 50%; filter: blur(90px); opacity: .13; animation: drift 18s ease-in-out infinite alternate; }
.nebula-one { top: -25%; right: -25%; background: var(--accent); }
.nebula-two { bottom: -35%; left: -25%; background: var(--x); animation-delay: -6s; }
.nebula-three { top: 30%; right: -36%; background: var(--o); opacity: .065; animation-delay: -12s; }
.space-grain { position: absolute; inset: 0; opacity: .17; background-image: radial-gradient(#8491b1 0.6px, transparent .6px); background-size: 43px 43px; mask-image: radial-gradient(ellipse, transparent 25%, black); }
.star { position: absolute; width: 2px; height: 2px; background: #cbd3ff; border-radius: 50%; opacity: .2; animation: twinkle 6s ease-in-out infinite; }
.star:nth-of-type(4n) { width: 3px; height: 3px; box-shadow: 0 0 9px var(--x); }
.icon-button { display: inline-flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center; width: 46px; height: 46px; padding: 0; flex-shrink: 0; border-radius: 15px; background: #ffffff04; border: 1px solid var(--line); color: var(--muted); }
.icon-button:active { background: #ffffff0d; color: var(--text); }
.button { min-height: 48px; border-radius: 16px; border: 1px solid transparent; display: flex; gap: 10px; align-items: center; justify-content: center; padding: 12px 20px; font-weight: 650; font-size: 14px; color: var(--text); }
.button-primary { background: linear-gradient(105deg, color-mix(in srgb, var(--accent) 70%, #29245c), color-mix(in srgb, var(--accent) 80%, #211638)); border-color: #c3b2ff35; box-shadow: 0 6px 30px color-mix(in srgb, var(--accent) 21%, transparent), inset 0 1px 0 #ffffff22; color: #fff; }
.button-secondary { background: #ffffff04; border-color: #ffffff14; color: #d4d6e8; }
.button-text { background: transparent; color: var(--muted); font-weight: 500; font-size: 12px; min-height: 44px; padding: 10px 12px; }
.button-danger { background: #82324b; border-color: #f6a1b836; }
.danger-text { color: #f3a1b5; }
.brand-dot { display: inline-block; width: 6px; height: 6px; border-radius: 50%; background: var(--x); box-shadow: 0 0 12px var(--x); flex-shrink: 0; }
.start-screen { width: min(100%, 480px); margin: auto; min-height: 100svh; padding: max(24px, env(safe-area-inset-top)) 28px max(22px, env(safe-area-inset-bottom)); display: flex; flex-direction: column; user-select: none; }
.start-header { display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: space-between; }
.brand-mini { display: flex; gap: 9px; align-items: center; font-size: 10px; color: var(--muted); }
.start-main { display: flex; flex-direction: column; align-items: center; width: 100%; padding: 40px 0 32px; margin: auto 0; }
.eyebrow { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 7px; font-size: 10px; color: #b6abc8; letter-spacing: .1px; }
.hero-emblem { position: relative; width: 252px; height: 216px; margin: 8px 0 14px; direction: ltr; }
.orbit { position: absolute; border: 1px solid #ffffff0b; border-radius: 50%; top: 10px; left: 20px; width: 212px; height: 196px; transform: rotate(-30deg); }
.orbit-one { height: 138px; top: 40px; width: 252px; left: 0; border-color: color-mix(in srgb, var(--accent) 20%, transparent); }
.orbit-two { width: 190px; height: 190px; left: 32px; border-style: dashed; border-color: #ffffff0a; }
.hero-mark { position: absolute; width: 130px; height: 130px; top: 43px; border-radius: 32px; display: grid; place-items: center; }
.mark-x { left: 9px; transform: rotate(-12deg); background: radial-gradient(circle, color-mix(in srgb, var(--x) 10%, transparent), transparent 70%); }
.mark-o { right: 7px; top: 55px; transform: rotate(10deg); background: radial-gradient(circle, color-mix(in srgb, var(--o) 10%, transparent), transparent 70%); }
.hero-mark .player-symbol { width: 130px; filter: drop-shadow(0 0 13px currentColor); }
.orbital-dot { position: absolute; top: 22px; left: 92px; width: 5px; height: 5px; border-radius: 50%; background: var(--x); box-shadow: 0 0 12px var(--x); }
.emblem-spark { position: absolute; bottom: 22px; right: 43px; color: var(--o); font-size: 13px; }
h1 { font-size: clamp(36px, 8.8vw, 46px); font-weight: 850; line-height: 1.6; letter-spacing: -1.5px; }
h1 > span { color: var(--x); font-size: .75em; vertical-align: .06em; padding: 0 3px; text-shadow: 0 0 22px color-mix(in srgb, var(--x) 35%, transparent); }
.start-subtitle { font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.8; color: var(--muted); margin-top: 2px; }
.start-actions { display: grid; gap: 11px; margin-top: 34px; width: 100%; }
.start-actions .button { min-height: 54px; }
.start-cta { position: relative; font-size: 16px; }
.start-cta svg { position: absolute; left: 22px; }
.stats-preview { display: grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(3, 1fr); width: 100%; margin-top: 30px; padding-top: 24px; border-top: 1px solid var(--line); }
.stats-preview > div, .stat-trio > div { display: flex; flex-direction: column; align-items: center; gap: 8px; }
.stats-preview > div + div { border-right: 1px solid var(--line); }
.preview-label { color: var(--muted); font-size: 10px; display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 5px; }
.preview-label > span { font-size: 16px; line-height: 12px; }
.stats-preview strong { font-size: 25px; font-weight: 600; line-height: 1.2; font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums; }
.start-footer { display: flex; gap: 8px; align-items: center; justify-content: center; color: var(--muted); opacity: .8; font-size: 10px; }
.game-screen { width: min(100%, 470px); margin: auto; min-height: 100svh; padding: max(20px, env(safe-area-inset-top)) 24px max(16px, env(safe-area-inset-bottom)); display: flex; flex-direction: column; user-select: none; }
.game-header { display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: space-between; min-height: 48px; }
.game-header > div { display: flex; flex-direction: column; align-items: center; gap: 5px; }
.small-brand { color: var(--muted); font-size: 10px; }
.round-label { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 7px; font-size: 14px; }
.round-label b { font-size: 12px; color: var(--x); font-weight: 500; }
.match-content { width: 100%; margin: auto 0; padding-top: 24px; display: flex; flex-direction: column; align-items: center; }
.players-row { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 14px; width: 100%; }
.player-card { --player: var(--x); background: linear-gradient(135deg, color-mix(in srgb, var(--player) 4%, transparent), #ffffff02); border: 1px solid #ffffff12; border-radius: 19px; padding: 10px 14px 12px; flex: 1; min-width: 0; position: relative; transition: border-color .3s, box-shadow .3s; }
.player-o { --player: var(--o); }
.player-card.is-active { border-color: color-mix(in srgb, var(--player) 48%, transparent); box-shadow: 0 0 25px color-mix(in srgb, var(--player) 7%, transparent), inset 0 0 18px color-mix(in srgb, var(--player) 4%, transparent); }
.player-card.is-active::after { content: ""; position: absolute; top: -1px; right: 28%; width: 44%; height: 2px; background: var(--player); border-radius: 50%; box-shadow: 0 0 12px var(--player); animation: active-pulse 3s ease-in-out infinite; }
.player-card-top { display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: space-between; height: 36px; }
.player-card-top .player-symbol { width: 36px; height: 36px; margin-right: -8px; }
.player-card-top > strong { font-weight: 500; font-size: 25px; color: var(--player); }
.player-name { font-size: 12px; color: #eaebf7; white-space: nowrap; overflow: hidden; text-overflow: ellipsis; margin: 5px 0; }
.active-dot { display: inline-block; width: 4px; height: 4px; border-radius: 50%; background: var(--muted); margin-left: 6px; vertical-align: middle; }
.is-active .active-dot { background: var(--player); box-shadow: 0 0 6px var(--player); }
.player-caption { font-size: 9px; color: var(--muted); }
.versus { font-size: 10px; color: #777b98; display: flex; flex-direction: column; align-items: center; gap: 3px; }
.versus > span { font-size: 9px; }
.turn-status { min-height: 54px; display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center; gap: 8px; font-size: 12px; text-align: center; max-width: 100%; overflow-wrap: anywhere; }
.status-dot { height: 6px; width: 6px; flex-shrink: 0; border-radius: 50%; background: var(--x); box-shadow: 0 0 10px var(--x); }
.turn-status[data-player="O"] .status-dot { background: var(--o); box-shadow: 0 0 10px var(--o); }
.turn-status .status-dot.status-draw { background: #b8a1ff; box-shadow: 0 0 10px #b8a1ff; }
.board-arena { width: min(100%, 360px); aspect-ratio: 1; position: relative; padding: 10px; border-radius: 28px; border: 1px solid #b5a5ff1c; background: linear-gradient(150deg, #ffffff04, #ffffff01); box-shadow: 0 18px 70px #00000033, 0 0 50px color-mix(in srgb, var(--accent) 5%, transparent); }
.board-grid { display: grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(3, minmax(0, 1fr)); grid-template-rows: repeat(3, minmax(0, 1fr)); height: 100%; width: 100%; }
.game-cell { position: relative; margin: 4px; min-height: 44px; min-width: 44px; padding: 0; border: 1px solid #c9c7ff12; border-radius: 17px; background: linear-gradient(140deg, #b4b4ff08, #17182c32); display: grid; place-items: center; }
.game-cell[aria-disabled="true"] { cursor: default; }
.game-cell[aria-disabled="true"]:active { transform: none; }
.game-cell .player-symbol { width: 90%; height: 90%; filter: drop-shadow(0 0 7px currentColor); }
.cell-point { width: 3px; height: 3px; border-radius: 50%; background: #b5b4de29; }
.cell-x { border-color: color-mix(in srgb, var(--x) 20%, transparent); background: color-mix(in srgb, var(--x) 4%, transparent); }
.cell-o { border-color: color-mix(in srgb, var(--o) 20%, transparent); background: color-mix(in srgb, var(--o) 4%, transparent); }
.cell-winning.cell-x { box-shadow: inset 0 0 28px color-mix(in srgb, var(--x) 12%, transparent); border-color: color-mix(in srgb, var(--x) 50%, transparent); }
.cell-winning.cell-o { box-shadow: inset 0 0 28px color-mix(in srgb, var(--o) 12%, transparent); border-color: color-mix(in srgb, var(--o) 50%, transparent); }
.arena-corner { width: 22px; height: 22px; position: absolute; pointer-events: none; border-color: #bba6ff65; }
.corner-one { top: -1px; right: -1px; border-top: 1px solid; border-right: 1px solid; border-radius: 0 27px 0 0; }
.corner-two { bottom: -1px; left: -1px; border-bottom: 1px solid; border-left: 1px solid; border-radius: 0 0 0 27px; }
.winning-line { position: absolute; pointer-events: none; inset: 10px; width: calc(100% - 20px); height: calc(100% - 20px); filter: drop-shadow(0 0 8px currentColor); }
.celebration { position: absolute; top: 50%; left: 50%; pointer-events: none; }
.celebration i { width: 4px; height: 4px; position: absolute; background: currentColor; box-shadow: 0 0 6px currentColor; border-radius: 1px; }
.arena-draw { animation: draw-pulse 1s ease-out; }
.board-caption { margin: 17px 0 20px; text-align: center; font-size: 10px; color: var(--muted); }
.game-controls { display: flex; gap: 10px; width: min(100%, 360px); }
.game-controls .button { flex: 1; }
.game-controls .icon-button { width: 50px; height: 50px; }
.reset-match { margin-top: 7px; }
.game-footer { display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center; gap: 8px; font-size: 9px; color: var(--muted); margin-top: 20px; }
.game-footer .brand-dot { width: 4px; height: 4px; opacity: .65; }
.sheet-dialog { position: fixed; inset: auto 0 0; margin: 0 auto; padding: 0; width: min(100%, 480px); max-width: 100%; max-height: 90dvh; border: 0; border-radius: 28px 28px 0 0; color: var(--text); background: var(--panel); overflow-y: auto; overscroll-behavior: contain; box-shadow: 0 -20px 80px #00000066; }
.sheet-dialog::backdrop { background: #02030cd9; backdrop-filter: blur(8px); }
.sheet-surface { padding: 12px 24px max(24px, env(safe-area-inset-bottom)); border: 1px solid #bfb0ff1a; border-bottom: 0; border-radius: inherit; }
.sheet-handle { width: 32px; height: 3px; background: #ffffff2a; border-radius: 3px; margin: 0 auto 15px; }
.sheet-header { display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: space-between; gap: 12px; }
.sheet-header h2 { font-size: 21px; font-weight: 750; }
.sheet-header .icon-button { background: transparent; border: 0; width: 44px; height: 44px; margin-left: -10px; }
.sheet-description { color: var(--muted); font-size: 12px; line-height: 1.9; margin: 6px 0 21px; }
.sheet-actions { display: grid; gap: 10px; margin-top: 26px; }
.settings-section { margin-top: 23px; }
.settings-section legend { color: #dcddee; font-size: 12px; margin-bottom: 12px; }
.name-fields { display: flex; gap: 12px; }
.name-field { width: 50%; min-width: 0; }
.name-field > span { display: block; font-size: 10px; margin-bottom: 8px; }
.name-field input { width: 100%; min-height: 48px; font-size: 14px; border: 1px solid #ffffff18; background: #ffffff04; color: var(--text); border-radius: 12px; padding: 10px 12px; }
.toggle-row { display: flex; justify-content: space-between; align-items: center; gap: 12px; padding: 13px 0; border-bottom: 1px solid var(--line); }
.toggle-row > span { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 10px; font-size: 13px; }
.toggle-row > span > svg { color: var(--muted); }
.toggle-row small { display: block; color: var(--muted); font-size: 9px; margin-top: 5px; }
.toggle { width: 48px; height: 44px; padding: 0; flex-shrink: 0; border: 0; background: transparent; position: relative; }
.toggle::before { content: ""; position: absolute; inset: 9px 0; border-radius: 20px; background: #ffffff22; transition: background .2s; }
.toggle > span { position: absolute; top: 13px; left: 5px; border-radius: 50%; height: 18px; width: 18px; background: #d2d4e5; transition: transform .2s; }
.toggle[aria-checked="true"]::before { background: var(--accent); }
.toggle[aria-checked="true"] > span { transform: translateX(20px); background: white; }
.theme-options { display: grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(3, minmax(0, 1fr)); gap: 9px; }
.theme-option { position: relative; padding: 6px 6px 10px; border: 1px solid #ffffff13; border-radius: 12px; cursor: pointer; text-align: center; font-size: 10px; }
.theme-option input { position: absolute; opacity: 0; width: 100%; height: 100%; inset: 0; margin: 0; cursor: pointer; }
.theme-option:has(:focus-visible) { outline: 2px solid var(--x); outline-offset: 3px; }
.theme-selected { border-color: var(--accent); background: color-mix(in srgb, var(--accent) 8%, transparent); }
.theme-swatch { display: flex; height: 48px; border-radius: 7px; gap: 8px; align-items: center; justify-content: center; margin-bottom: 9px; background: #111126; }
.theme-swatch i { height: 15px; width: 15px; border-radius: 50%; background: #35dfff; box-shadow: 0 0 12px #35dfff66; }
.theme-swatch i + i { background: #ff65ca; box-shadow: 0 0 12px #ff65ca66; }
.theme-swatch svg { position: absolute; left: 6px; top: 6px; background: #111126; border-radius: 50%; color: white; }
.theme-preview-sunset .theme-swatch { background: #321734; }
.theme-preview-sunset i { background: #ffb773; box-shadow: 0 0 12px #ffb77366; }
.theme-preview-sunset i + i { background: #f58aff; }
.theme-preview-ice .theme-swatch { background: #102e48; }
.theme-preview-ice i { background: #75eeff; }
.theme-preview-ice i + i { background: #b5b9ff; }
.save-note { text-align: center; color: var(--muted); font-size: 10px; margin: 23px 0 5px; }
.sheet-surface > .button-text { margin: 10px auto 0; }
.result-emblem { position: relative; width: 112px; height: 112px; margin: 14px auto 20px; border-radius: 50%; display: grid; place-items: center; background: radial-gradient(circle, #a995ff15, transparent 75%); }
.result-emblem .player-symbol { width: 110px; height: 110px; filter: drop-shadow(0 0 13px currentColor); }
.result-emblem > span { font-size: 70px; line-height: 1; color: #c7adff; }
.result-trophy { position: absolute; right: -1px; bottom: 5px; color: #eed5a3; }
.result-copy { text-align: center; }
.result-copy h3 { font-size: 24px; margin-bottom: 10px; overflow-wrap: anywhere; }
.result-copy p { font-size: 12px; color: var(--muted); }
.confirm-emblem { display: grid; place-items: center; margin: 15px auto; width: 60px; height: 60px; border-radius: 20px; color: #e3a8c2; background: #e3a8c20a; }
.compact-dialog .sheet-description { text-align: center; }
.empty-stats { text-align: center; padding: 34px 10px; }
.empty-stats > svg { color: var(--accent); margin: 0 auto 20px; }
.empty-stats h3 { font-size: 18px; margin-bottom: 10px; }
.empty-stats p { color: var(--muted); font-size: 12px; line-height: 1.8; }
.total-rounds { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 12px; padding: 17px; border-radius: 15px; background: #ffffff04; border: 1px solid var(--line); font-size: 12px; }
.total-rounds strong { font-size: 30px; margin-right: auto; font-weight: 550; }
.total-rounds svg { color: var(--accent); }
.stat-trio { display: grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(3, 1fr); margin: 23px 0; font-size: 10px; color: var(--muted); }
.stat-trio strong { font-size: 25px; color: var(--text); font-weight: 600; }
.stat-trio strong.text-x { color: var(--x); }
.stat-trio strong.text-o { color: var(--o); }
.comparison { border-top: 1px solid var(--line); padding-top: 20px; }
.comparison h3 { font-size: 13px; margin-bottom: 15px; }
.comparison-labels { display: flex; gap: 12px; justify-content: space-between; font-size: 10px; color: var(--muted); }
.comparison-labels > span { min-width: 0; overflow: hidden; text-overflow: ellipsis; white-space: nowrap; }
.comparison-bar { display: flex; gap: 0; height: 8px; border-radius: 5px; overflow: hidden; background: #ffffff12; margin: 12px 0; }
.bar-x { background: var(--x); }
.bar-o { background: var(--o); }
.bar-draw { background: #555571; }
.streak-grid { display: grid; grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr; gap: 12px; margin: 23px 0 10px; }
.streak-grid > div { display: flex; flex-direction: column; align-items: center; gap: 7px; border: 1px solid var(--line); border-radius: 15px; padding: 17px 10px; min-width: 0; }
.streak-grid svg { color: #d8b084; margin-bottom: 4px; }
.streak-grid span { font-size: 11px; }
.streak-grid strong { font-size: 25px; font-weight: 550; }
.streak-grid small { font-size: 9px; color: var(--muted); max-width: 100%; overflow: hidden; text-overflow: ellipsis; white-space: nowrap; }
.storage-notice { position: fixed; bottom: max(8px, env(safe-area-inset-bottom)); left: 12px; right: 12px; width: fit-content; max-width: calc(100% - 24px); margin: auto; padding: 9px 14px; background: #262136; border: 1px solid #ffffff20; border-radius: 10px; font-size: 10px; z-index: 5; }
@keyframes drift { to { transform: translate3d(25px, 45px, 0) scale(1.08); } }
@keyframes twinkle { 50% { opacity: .65; } }
@keyframes active-pulse { 50% { opacity: .45; } }
@keyframes draw-pulse { 50% { box-shadow: 0 0 50px #aa80ff38, inset 0 0 25px #aa80ff15; border-color: #aa80ff88; } }
@media (hover: hover) {
  .button-primary:hover { box-shadow: 0 8px 40px color-mix(in srgb, var(--accent) 34%, transparent); filter: brightness(1.1); }
  .button-secondary:hover, .icon-button:hover { background: #ffffff09; border-color: #ffffff25; }
  .cell-empty[aria-disabled="false"]:hover { background: #ffffff08; border-color: #ffffff32; }
}
@media (min-width: 640px) {
  .sheet-dialog { inset: 0; margin: auto; border-radius: 25px; height: fit-content; max-height: 88dvh; }
  .sheet-surface { border-bottom: 1px solid #bfb0ff1a; }
  .sheet-handle { display: none; }
  .sheet-surface { padding-top: 22px; }
  .neon-background { background-image: radial-gradient(ellipse at 50% 45%, color-mix(in srgb, var(--accent) 7%, transparent), transparent 65%); }
  .nebula-one { right: -10%; }
  .nebula-two { left: -15%; }
  .nebula-three { right: -15%; }
}
@media (max-height: 800px) {
  .start-main { padding: 20px 0; }
  .hero-emblem { height: 180px; transform: scale(.87); margin: -5px 0 8px; }
  .start-actions { margin-top: 24px; }
  .stats-preview { margin-top: 22px; padding-top: 18px; }
  .game-screen { padding-top: max(12px, env(safe-area-inset-top)); padding-bottom: max(12px, env(safe-area-inset-bottom)); }
  .match-content { padding-top: 14px; }
  .player-card { padding: 7px 12px 9px; }
  .player-card-top { height: 28px; }
  .player-name { margin: 3px 0; }
  .turn-status { min-height: 44px; }
  .board-arena { width: min(100%, 340px, calc(100dvh - 400px)); min-width: 180px; }
  .board-caption { margin: 12px 0; }
  .game-footer { display: none; }
}
@media (max-height: 680px) {
  .start-screen { padding-top: max(14px, env(safe-area-inset-top)); padding-bottom: max(14px, env(safe-area-inset-bottom)); }
  .start-main { padding: 12px 0 16px; }
  .hero-emblem { height: 145px; transform: scale(.73); margin-top: -10px; margin-bottom: 8px; }
  .eyebrow { font-size: 9px; }
  .start-actions { margin-top: 18px; gap: 8px; }
  .start-actions .button { min-height: 48px; }
  .stats-preview { margin-top: 18px; padding-top: 14px; }
  .board-caption { display: none; }
  .game-controls { margin-top: 16px; }
  .turn-status { min-height: 40px; }
  .player-caption { display: none; }
  .board-arena { width: min(100%, 320px, calc(100dvh - 370px)); }
}
@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {
  *, *::before, *::after { animation: none !important; transition: none !important; scroll-behavior: auto !important; }
  .nebula { filter: blur(70px); }
}

### components/GameApp.tsx

```tsx
"use client";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { AnimatePresence, MotionConfig, motion, useReducedMotion } from "framer-motion";
import { Plus, RotateCcw, Volume2, VolumeX } from "lucide-react";
import { ConfirmDialog, type Confirmation } from "@/components/ConfirmDialog";
import { GameBoard } from "@/components/GameBoard";
import { GameHeader } from "@/components/GameHeader";
import { IconButton } from "@/components/IconButton";
import { NeonBackground } from "@/components/NeonBackground";
import { PlayerCard } from "@/components/PlayerCard";
import { ResultModal } from "@/components/ResultModal";
import { SettingsSheet } from "@/components/SettingsSheet";
import { StartScreen } from "@/components/StartScreen";
import { StatisticsSheet } from "@/components/StatisticsSheet";
import { useGame } from "@/hooks/useGame";
import { useHapticFeedback } from "@/hooks/useHapticFeedback";
import { useLocalStorage } from "@/hooks/useLocalStorage";
import { useSound } from "@/hooks/useSound";
import { DEFAULT_SETTINGS, STORAGE_KEYS } from "@/lib/constants";
import { gameReducer } from "@/lib/gameLogic";
import { parseSettings } from "@/lib/storage";

export function GameApp() {
  const { state, dispatch, ready: gameReady, storageFailed } = useGame();
  const { value: settings, setValue: setSettings, ready: settingsReady, failed } = useLocalStorage(STORAGE_KEYS.settings, DEFAULT_SETTINGS, parseSettings);
  const [sheet, setSheet] = useState<"settings" | "stats" | null>(null);
  const [confirmation, setConfirmation] = useState<Confirmation | null>(null);
  const [resultVisible, setResultVisible] = useState(false);
  const reduced = useReducedMotion();
  const sound = useSound(settings.sound);
  const haptic = useHapticFeedback(settings.haptics);
  const { match, stats } = state;
  const ready = gameReady && settingsReady;
  const names = { X: settings.names.X.trim() || DEFAULT_SETTINGS.names.X, O: settings.names.O.trim() || DEFAULT_SETTINGS.names.O };
  const inProgress = match.status === "playing" && match.board.some(Boolean);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (match.status === "playing") return;
    const timer = window.setTimeout(() => setResultVisible(true), reduced ? 80 : 780);
    return () => window.clearTimeout(timer);
  }, [match.status, match.round, reduced]);
  const next = () => { setResultVisible(false); dispatch({ type: "next" }); sound("start"); };
  const reset = () => {
    const action = () => { setResultVisible(false); dispatch({ type: "reset-match" }); sound("start"); };
    if (match.board.some(Boolean) || match.round > 1) setConfirmation({ title: "نبدأ مباراة جديدة؟", message: "ستُصفَّر نتائج المباراة الحالية. تبقى إنجازاتكما في الإحصاءات محفوظة.", action: "إعادة المباراة", onConfirm: action });
    else action();
  };
  const leave = () => {
    const action = () => { setResultVisible(false); dispatch({ type: "leave" }); };
    if (inProgress) setConfirmation({ title: "تغادران الساحة؟", message: "ستنتهي المباراة ولن تُحتسب الجولة غير المكتملة. تبقى الإحصاءات السابقة محفوظة.", action: "العودة للرئيسية", onConfirm: action });
    else action();
  };
  const select = (index: number) => {
    const updated = gameReducer(state, { type: "move", index });
    if (updated === state) return;
    dispatch({ type: "move", index });
    sound(updated.match.status === "won" ? "win" : updated.match.status === "draw" ? "draw" : "move");
    haptic(updated.match.status === "won");
  };
  const statusText = match.status === "won" && match.winner ? `الفوز من نصيب ${names[match.winner]}` : match.status === "draw" ? "تعادل ذكي — الساحة متكافئة" : `دور ${names[match.currentPlayer]} الآن`;
  return <MotionConfig reducedMotion="user"><main className="app-shell" data-theme={settings.theme}>
    <NeonBackground />
    <AnimatePresence mode="wait" initial={false}>
      {state.screen === "start" ? <StartScreen key="start" stats={stats} ready={ready} onStart={() => { dispatch({ type: "start" }); sound("start"); }} onSettings={() => setSheet("settings")} onStats={() => setSheet("stats")} /> : <motion.section key="game" className="game-screen" aria-label="ساحة اللعب" initial={reduced ? false : { opacity: 0, y: 12 }} animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }} exit={{ opacity: 0 }}>
        <GameHeader round={match.round} onBack={leave} onSettings={() => setSheet("settings")} />
        <div className="match-content">
          <div className="players-row"><PlayerCard player="X" name={names.X} wins={match.scores.X} active={match.status === "playing" && match.currentPlayer === "X"} /><span className="versus" aria-hidden="true">×<span>ضد</span>○</span><PlayerCard player="O" name={names.O} wins={match.scores.O} active={match.status === "playing" && match.currentPlayer === "O"} /></div>
          <div className="turn-status" data-player={match.winner ?? match.currentPlayer} role="status" aria-live="polite" aria-atomic="true"><span className={match.status === "draw" ? "status-dot status-draw" : "status-dot"} />{statusText}</div>
          <GameBoard board={match.board} status={match.status} winner={match.winner} onSelect={select} />
          <p className="board-caption">{match.status === "playing" ? "ثلاث علامات على خط واحد. والانتصار لك." : "جولة انتهت، وتحدٍّ جديد ينتظركما."}</p>
          <div className="game-controls"><button className="button button-primary" onClick={() => { if (inProgress) setConfirmation({ title: "نبدأ الجولة من جديد؟", message: "ستُلغى حركات هذه الجولة دون احتساب نتيجة. يبقى اللاعب الذي بدأها أولًا.", action: "جولة جديدة", onConfirm: next }); else next(); }}><Plus size={20} /> جولة جديدة</button><IconButton label={settings.sound ? "كتم الصوت" : "تشغيل الصوت"} aria-pressed={settings.sound} onClick={() => setSettings({ ...settings, sound: !settings.sound })}>{settings.sound ? <Volume2 size={20} /> : <VolumeX size={20} />}</IconButton></div>
          <button className="button button-text reset-match" onClick={reset}><RotateCcw size={15} /> إعادة المباراة</button>
        </div>
        <footer className="game-footer"><span className="brand-dot" /> لحظة تركيز. حركة تصنع الفرق.</footer>
      </motion.section>}
    </AnimatePresence>
    {failed || storageFailed ? <p className="storage-notice" role="status">الحفظ غير متاح على هذا الجهاز. يمكنكما متابعة اللعب.</p> : null}
    <ResultModal open={state.screen === "game" && resultVisible && sheet === null} winner={match.winner} name={match.winner ? names[match.winner] : ""} round={match.round} onClose={() => setResultVisible(false)} onNext={next} onReset={reset} />
    <SettingsSheet open={sheet === "settings"} settings={settings} onChange={setSettings} onClose={() => setSheet(null)} onReset={() => setConfirmation({ title: "استعادة الإعدادات؟", message: "ستعود أسماء اللاعبين والصوت والاهتزاز والألوان إلى إعداداتها الأصلية.", action: "استعادة الافتراضي", onConfirm: () => setSettings(DEFAULT_SETTINGS) })} />
    <StatisticsSheet open={sheet === "stats"} stats={stats} settings={{ ...settings, names }} onClose={() => setSheet(null)} onReset={() => setConfirmation({ title: "تصفير سجل الساحة؟", message: "ستُحذف جميع الإحصاءات وسلاسل الانتصارات المحفوظة على هذا الجهاز. لا يمكن التراجع عن الحذف.", action: "تصفير الإحصاءات", onConfirm: () => dispatch({ type: "reset-stats" }) })} />
    <ConfirmDialog confirmation={confirmation} onClose={() => setConfirmation(null)} />
  </main></MotionConfig>;
}
```

### components/GameBoard.tsx

```tsx
"use client";

import { motion, useReducedMotion } from "framer-motion";
import { GameCell } from "@/components/GameCell";
import { getWinningLine } from "@/lib/gameLogic";
import type { Board, GameStatus, Player } from "@/types/game";

export function GameBoard({ board, status, winner, onSelect }: { board: Board; status: GameStatus; winner: Player | null; onSelect: (index: number) => void }) {
  const line = getWinningLine(board);
  const reduced = useReducedMotion();
  // Grid and SVG share an explicit left-to-right coordinate system inside the RTL app.
  const point = (index: number) => ({ x: (index % 3) * 100 + 50, y: Math.floor(index / 3) * 100 + 50 });
  const first = line ? point(line[0]) : null;
  const last = line ? point(line[2]) : null;
  return <div className={`board-arena ${status === "draw" ? "arena-draw" : ""} ${winner ? `arena-${winner.toLowerCase()}` : ""}`}>
    <span className="arena-corner corner-one" aria-hidden="true" /><span className="arena-corner corner-two" aria-hidden="true" />
    <div className="board-grid" role="group" aria-label="لوحة اللعب، تسع خانات" dir="ltr">
      {board.map((value, index) => <GameCell key={index} index={index} value={value} locked={status !== "playing"} winning={line?.includes(index) ?? false} onSelect={onSelect} />)}
    </div>
    {first && last ? <svg className={`winning-line text-${winner?.toLowerCase()}`} viewBox="0 0 300 300" aria-hidden="true"><motion.line x1={first.x} y1={first.y} x2={last.x} y2={last.y} stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="4" strokeLinecap="round" initial={reduced ? false : { pathLength: 0 }} animate={{ pathLength: 1 }} transition={{ duration: 0.4, delay: 0.12 }} /></svg> : null}
    {winner && !reduced ? <div className={`celebration text-${winner.toLowerCase()}`} aria-hidden="true">{Array.from({ length: 12 }, (_, index) => <motion.i key={index} initial={{ x: 0, y: 0, opacity: 1, scale: 0.4 }} animate={{ x: Math.cos(index * Math.PI / 6) * 155, y: Math.sin(index * Math.PI / 6) * 155, opacity: 0, scale: 1.2 }} transition={{ duration: 0.8, delay: 0.1 }} />)}</div> : null}
  </div>;
}
```

"use client";

import { motion, useReducedMotion } from "framer-motion";
import { PlayerSymbol } from "@/components/PlayerSymbol";
import type { CellValue } from "@/types/game";

export function GameCell({ value, index, locked, winning, onSelect }: { value: CellValue; index: number; locked: boolean; winning: boolean; onSelect: (index: number) => void }) {
  const reduced = useReducedMotion();
  const symbol = value === "X" ? "×" : "○";
  return <motion.button type="button" className={`game-cell ${value ? `cell-${value.toLowerCase()}` : "cell-empty"} ${winning ? "cell-winning" : ""}`} data-cell={index} aria-label={`الصف ${Math.floor(index / 3) + 1}، العمود ${index % 3 + 1}، ${value ? symbol : "خانة فارغة"}`} aria-disabled={locked || value !== null} onClick={() => { if (!locked && !value) onSelect(index); }} whileTap={!locked && !value && !reduced ? { scale: 0.95 } : undefined}>
    {value ? <PlayerSymbol player={value} /> : <span className="cell-point" aria-hidden="true" />}
  </motion.button>;
}

"use client";

import { motion, useReducedMotion } from "framer-motion";
import type { Player } from "@/types/game";

export function PlayerSymbol({ player, animated = true, className = "" }: { player: Player; animated?: boolean; className?: string }) {
  const reduced = useReducedMotion();
  const initial = animated && !reduced ? { pathLength: 0, opacity: 0 } : false;
  return (
    <svg viewBox="0 0 100 100" fill="none" aria-hidden="true" className={`player-symbol symbol-${player.toLowerCase()} ${className}`}>
      {player === "X" ? <>
        <motion.path d="M28 28L72 72" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="8" strokeLinecap="round" initial={initial} animate={{ pathLength: 1, opacity: 1 }} transition={{ duration: reduced ? 0 : 0.24 }} />
        <motion.path d="M72 28L28 72" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="8" strokeLinecap="round" initial={initial} animate={{ pathLength: 1, opacity: 1 }} transition={{ duration: reduced ? 0 : 0.24, delay: reduced ? 0 : 0.1 }} />
      </> : <motion.circle cx="50" cy="50" r="27" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="7" strokeLinecap="round" initial={initial} animate={{ pathLength: 1, opacity: 1 }} transition={{ duration: reduced ? 0 : 0.4 }} />}
    </svg>
  );
}

"use client";

import { motion, useReducedMotion } from "framer-motion";
import { ArrowLeft, ChartNoAxesCombined, Settings2, Sparkles, UsersRound } from "lucide-react";
import { IconButton } from "@/components/IconButton";
import { PlayerSymbol } from "@/components/PlayerSymbol";
import { formatNumber } from "@/lib/utils";
import type { GameStats } from "@/types/game";

interface Props { stats: GameStats; ready: boolean; onStart: () => void; onSettings: () => void; onStats: () => void }
export function StartScreen({ stats, ready, onStart, onSettings, onStats }: Props) {
  const reduced = useReducedMotion();
  const item = { hidden: { opacity: 0, y: reduced ? 0 : 16 }, show: { opacity: 1, y: 0 } };
  return <motion.section className="start-screen" aria-label="الشاشة الرئيسية" initial="hidden" animate="show" exit={{ opacity: 0 }} variants={{ show: { transition: { staggerChildren: reduced ? 0 : 0.09 } } }}>
    <header className="start-header">
      <div className="brand-mini"><span className="brand-dot" /> مساحة صغيرة. تحدٍّ كبير.</div>
      <IconButton label="الإعدادات" onClick={onSettings} disabled={!ready}><Settings2 size={20} /></IconButton>
    </header>
    <div className="start-main">
      <motion.div variants={item} className="eyebrow"><Sparkles size={13} /> الساحة لكما</motion.div>
      <motion.div variants={item} className="hero-emblem" aria-hidden="true">
        <div className="orbit orbit-one" /><div className="orbit orbit-two" />
        <div className="hero-mark mark-x"><PlayerSymbol player="X" /></div>
        <div className="hero-mark mark-o"><PlayerSymbol player="O" /></div>
        <span className="orbital-dot" /><span className="emblem-spark">✦</span>
      </motion.div>
      <motion.h1 variants={item}>نقطة <span>×</span> دائرة</motion.h1>
      <motion.p variants={item} className="start-subtitle">تحدَّ صديقك في ساحة النيون</motion.p>
      <motion.div variants={item} className="start-actions">
        <button className="button button-primary start-cta" onClick={onStart} disabled={!ready}>ابدأ الجولة <ArrowLeft size={21} /></button>
        <button className="button button-secondary" onClick={onStats} disabled={!ready}><ChartNoAxesCombined size={19} /> الإحصاءات</button>
      </motion.div>
      <motion.div variants={item} className="stats-preview" aria-label="ملخص الإحصاءات">
        <div><span className="preview-label"><span className="text-x">×</span> انتصارات</span><strong className="text-x">{formatNumber(stats.wins.X)}</strong></div>
        <div><span className="preview-label">تعادلات</span><strong>{formatNumber(stats.draws)}</strong></div>
        <div><span className="preview-label"><span className="text-o">○</span> انتصارات</span><strong className="text-o">{formatNumber(stats.wins.O)}</strong></div>

    <motion.footer variants={item} className="start-footer"><UsersRound size={15} /><span>لاعبان · جهاز واحد · تحدٍّ بلا حدود</span></motion.footer>
  </motion.section>;
}

export function SettingsSheet({ open, settings, onChange, onClose, onReset }: Props) {

"×" : "○"}</span><input aria-label={`اسم اللاعب ${player === "X" ? "×" : "○"}`} value={settings.names[player]} maxLength={24} onChange={event => changeName(player, event.target.value)} onBlur={() => changeName(player, settings.names[player].trim() || DEFAULT_SETTINGS.names[player])} autoComplete="off" dir="auto" /></label>)}

    </div>
    <fieldset className="settings-section"><legend>اختر أجواء الساحة</legend><div className="theme-options">{THEMES.map(theme => <label key={theme.id} className={`theme-option theme-preview-${theme.id} ${settings.theme === theme.id ? "theme-selected" : ""}`}><input type="radio" name="theme" value={theme.id} checked={settings.theme === theme.id} onChange={() => onChange({ ...settings, theme: theme.id })} /><span className="theme-swatch" aria-hidden="true"><i /><i />{settings.theme === theme.id ? <Check size={17} /> : null}</span><span>{theme.name}</span></label>)}</div></fieldset>
    <p className="save-note">تُطبَّق التغييرات وتُحفظ تلقائيًا على جهازك.</p>

  </Sheet>;
}

import { ChartNoAxesCombined, Flame, Sparkles, Trash2, Trophy } from "lucide-react";
import { Sheet } from "@/components/Sheet";
import { formatNumber, percentage } from "@/lib/utils";
import type { GameSettings, GameStats } from "@/types/game";

export function StatisticsSheet({ open, stats, settings, onClose, onReset }: { open: boolean; stats: GameStats; settings: GameSettings; onClose: () => void; onReset: () => void }) {
  const x = percentage(stats.wins.X, stats.totalRounds);
  const o = percentage(stats.wins.O, stats.totalRounds);
  return <Sheet open={open} title="سجل الساحة" titleId="stats-title" onClose={onClose}>
    <p className="sheet-description">كل جولة تترك أثرًا.</p>
    {stats.totalRounds === 0 ? <div className="empty-stats"><ChartNoAxesCombined size={42} /><h3>الساحة تنتظر أول انتصار</h3><p>أكملا جولتكما الأولى لتبدأ الحكاية هنا.</p></div> : <>
      <div className="total-rounds"><span>الجولات المكتملة</span><strong>{formatNumber(stats.totalRounds)}</strong><Sparkles size={22} /></div>
      <div className="stat-trio"><div><span className="text-x">انتصارات ×</span><strong className="text-x">{formatNumber(stats.wins.X)}</strong></div><div><span>تعادلات</span><strong>{formatNumber(stats.draws)}</strong></div><div><span className="text-o">انتصارات ○</span><strong className="text-o">{formatNumber(stats.wins.O)}</strong></div></div>
      <section className="comparison" aria-label="نسب الانتصارات"><h3>ميزان المنافسة</h3>
        <div className="comparison-labels"><span className="text-x">× <bdi>{settings.names.X}</bdi></span><span className="text-o">○ <bdi>{settings.names.O}</bdi></span></div>
        <div className="comparison-bar" role="img" aria-label={`فوز ×: ${x}%، فوز ○: ${o}%، والباقي تعادلات`}><span className="bar-x" style={{ width: `${stats.wins.X / stats.totalRounds * 100}%` }} /><span className="bar-draw" style={{ width: `${stats.draws / stats.totalRounds * 100}%` }} /><span className="bar-o" style={{ width: `${stats.wins.O / stats.totalRounds * 100}%` }} /></div>
        <div className="comparison-labels"><strong className="text-x">{x}%</strong><span>من إجمالي الجولات</span><strong className="text-o">{o}%</strong></div>
      </section>
      <div className="streak-grid"><div><Flame size={20} /><span>السلسلة الحالية</span><strong>{formatNumber(stats.streak.count)}</strong><small>{stats.streak.player ? settings.names[stats.streak.player] : "بانتظار انتصار جديد"}</small></div><div><Trophy size={20} /><span>أفضل سلسلة</span><strong>{formatNumber(stats.bestStreak.count)}</strong><small>{stats.bestStreak.player ? settings.names[stats.bestStreak.player] : "لم تبدأ بعد"}</small></div></div>
    </>}
    <button className="button button-text danger-text" onClick={onReset} disabled={stats.totalRounds === 0}><Trash2 size={16} /> تصفير الإحصاءات</button>
  </Sheet>;
}

### components/Sheet.tsx

```tsx
"use client";

import { useEffect, useRef, type ReactNode } from "react";
import { motion, useReducedMotion } from "framer-motion";
import { X } from "lucide-react";
import { IconButton } from "@/components/IconButton";

let openDialogs = 0;
let savedOverflow = "";

interface Props {
  open: boolean;
  title: string;
  titleId: string;
  onClose: () => void;
  children: ReactNode;
  compact?: boolean;
}
export function Sheet({ open, title, titleId, onClose, children, compact = false }: Props) {
  const ref = useRef<HTMLDialogElement>(null);
  const reduced = useReducedMotion();
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) return;
    const dialog = ref.current;
    const previous = document.activeElement;
    dialog?.showModal();
    if (openDialogs === 0) savedOverflow = document.body.style.overflow;
    openDialogs += 1;
    document.body.style.overflow = "hidden";
    return () => {
      dialog?.close();
      openDialogs -= 1;
      if (openDialogs === 0) document.body.style.overflow = savedOverflow;
      if (previous instanceof HTMLElement && previous.isConnected) {
        const parentDialog = previous.closest("dialog");
        if (!parentDialog || parentDialog.open) previous.focus();
      }
    };
  }, [open]);
  return <dialog ref={ref} className={`sheet-dialog ${compact ? "compact-dialog" : ""}`} aria-labelledby={titleId} onCancel={event => { event.preventDefault(); onClose(); }} onClick={event => { if (event.target === event.currentTarget) onClose(); }}>
    {open ? <motion.section className="sheet-surface" initial={reduced ? false : { y: 60, opacity: 0 }} animate={{ y: 0, opacity: 1 }} transition={{ type: "spring", damping: 30, stiffness: 320 }}>
      <div className="sheet-handle" aria-hidden="true" />
      <header className="sheet-header"><h2 id={titleId}>{title}</h2><IconButton label="إغلاق" onClick={onClose}><X size={19} /></IconButton></header>
      {children}
    </motion.section> : null}
  </dialog>;
}
```

### hooks/useGame.ts

```tsx
"use client";

import { startTransition, useEffect, useReducer, useState } from "react";
import { EMPTY_STATS, STORAGE_KEYS } from "@/lib/constants";
import { createGame, gameReducer } from "@/lib/gameLogic";
import { parseStats, readStorage, writeStorage } from "@/lib/storage";

export function useGame() {
  const [state, dispatch] = useReducer(gameReducer, undefined, createGame);
  const [ready, setReady] = useState(false);
  const [storageFailed, setStorageFailed] = useState(false);
  useEffect(() => {
    const loaded = readStorage(STORAGE_KEYS.stats, EMPTY_STATS, parseStats);
    startTransition(() => {
      dispatch({ type: "hydrate", stats: loaded.value });
      setStorageFailed(loaded.failed);
      setReady(true);
    });
  }, []);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (ready && !writeStorage(STORAGE_KEYS.stats, state.stats)) startTransition(() => setStorageFailed(true));
  }, [ready, state.stats]);
  return { state, dispatch, ready, storageFailed };
}
```

### hooks/useLocalStorage.ts

```tsx
"use client";

import { startTransition, useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { readStorage, writeStorage } from "@/lib/storage";

export function useLocalStorage<T>(key: string, fallback: T, parse: (value: unknown) => T) {
  const [value, setValue] = useState(fallback);
  const [ready, setReady] = useState(false);
  const [failed, setFailed] = useState(false);
  useEffect(() => {
    const loaded = readStorage(key, fallback, parse);
    startTransition(() => { setValue(loaded.value); setFailed(loaded.failed); setReady(true); });
  }, [key, fallback, parse]);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (ready && !writeStorage(key, value)) startTransition(() => setFailed(true));
  }, [key, value, ready]);
  return { value, setValue, ready, failed };
}
```

### hooks/useSound.ts

```tsx
"use client";

import { useCallback, useEffect, useRef } from "react";

type Sound = "move" | "win" | "draw" | "start";
export function useSound(enabled: boolean) {
  const context = useRef<AudioContext | null>(null);
  useEffect(() => () => {
    const active = context.current;
    context.current = null;
    if (active && active.state !== "closed") void active.close().catch(() => undefined);
  }, []);
  return useCallback((sound: Sound) => {
    if (!enabled) return;
    try {
      const Audio = window.AudioContext;
      if (!Audio) return;
      const audio = context.current ?? new Audio();
      context.current = audio;
      const play = () => {
        if (audio.state === "closed") return;
        const frequencies = sound === "win" ? [523.25, 659.25, 783.99, 1046.5] : sound === "draw" ? [392, 349.23, 329.63] : sound === "start" ? [392, 587.33] : [660];
        frequencies.forEach((frequency, index) => {
          const oscillator = audio.createOscillator();
          const gain = audio.createGain();
          const time = audio.currentTime + index * 0.095;
          oscillator.type = "sine";
          oscillator.frequency.setValueAtTime(frequency, time);
          gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0, time);
          gain.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(0.07, time + 0.008);
          gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, time + 0.18);
          oscillator.connect(gain);
          gain.connect(audio.destination);
          oscillator.start(time);
          oscillator.stop(time + 0.2);
          oscillator.onended = () => { oscillator.disconnect(); gain.disconnect(); };
        });
      };
      if (audio.state === "suspended") void audio.resume().then(play).catch(() => undefined);
      else play();
    } catch { /* Audio is optional; gameplay remains available. */ }
  }, [enabled]);
}
```

### hooks/useHapticFeedback.ts

```tsx
"use client";

import { useCallback } from "react";

export function useHapticFeedback(enabled: boolean) {
  return useCallback((celebrate = false) => {
    if (!enabled || typeof navigator.vibrate !== "function") return;
    try { navigator.vibrate(celebrate ? [20, 45, 30] : 12); }
    catch { /* Unsupported device policies must not interrupt a round. */ }
  }, [enabled]);
}
```

### lib/gameLogic.ts

```tsx
import { EMPTY_STATS, WINNING_LINES } from "@/lib/constants";
import type { Board, GameAction, GameState, GameStats, MatchState, Player, WinningLine } from "@/types/game";

export const otherPlayer = (player: Player): Player => player === "X" ? "O" : "X";
export const createBoard = (): Board => [null, null, null, null, null, null, null, null, null];

export function getWinningLine(board: Board): WinningLine | null {
  return WINNING_LINES.find(([a, b, c]) => board[a] !== null && board[a] === board[b] && board[a] === board[c]) ?? null;
}
export function checkWinner(board: Board): Player | null {
  const line = getWinningLine(board);
  return line ? board[line[0]] : null;
}
export function isBoardFull(board: Board): boolean {
  return board.every(cell => cell !== null);
}
export function getNextStartingPlayer(previousStarter: Player, winner: Player | null): Player {
  return otherPlayer(winner ?? previousStarter);
}
export function createMatch(): MatchState {
  return { board: createBoard(), currentPlayer: "X", startingPlayer: "X", status: "playing", winner: null, round: 1, scores: { X: 0, O: 0 } };
}
export function createGame(): GameState {
  return { screen: "start", match: createMatch(), stats: EMPTY_STATS };
}
export function recordResult(stats: GameStats, winner: Player | null): GameStats {
  const streak = winner
    ? { player: winner, count: stats.streak.player === winner ? stats.streak.count + 1 : 1 }
    : { player: null, count: 0 };
  return {
    totalRounds: stats.totalRounds + 1,
    wins: { X: stats.wins.X + Number(winner === "X"), O: stats.wins.O + Number(winner === "O") },
    draws: stats.draws + Number(winner === null),
    streak,
    bestStreak: streak.count > stats.bestStreak.count ? streak : stats.bestStreak,
  };
}
export function gameReducer(state: GameState, action: GameAction): GameState {
  const match = state.match;
  switch (action.type) {
    case "hydrate": return { ...state, stats: action.stats };
    case "start": return { ...state, screen: "game", match: createMatch() };
    case "leave": return { ...state, screen: "start", match: createMatch() };
    case "reset-match": return { ...state, match: createMatch() };
    case "reset-stats": return { ...state, stats: EMPTY_STATS };
    case "next": {
      const completed = match.status !== "playing";
      const starter = completed ? getNextStartingPlayer(match.startingPlayer, match.winner) : match.startingPlayer;
      return { ...state, match: { ...match, board: createBoard(), winner: null, status: "playing", startingPlayer: starter, currentPlayer: starter, round: match.round + Number(completed) } };
    }
    case "move": {
      const { index } = action;
      if (state.screen !== "game" || match.status !== "playing" || !Number.isInteger(index) || index < 0 || index > 8 || match.board[index] !== null) return state;
      const board: Board = [...match.board];
      board[index] = match.currentPlayer;
      const winner = checkWinner(board);
      const status = winner ? "won" : isBoardFull(board) ? "draw" : "playing";
      return {
        ...state,
        stats: status !== "playing" ? recordResult(state.stats, winner) : state.stats,
        match: {
          ...match, board, winner, status,
          currentPlayer: status === "playing" ? otherPlayer(match.currentPlayer) : match.currentPlayer,
          scores: winner ? { ...match.scores, [winner]: match.scores[winner] + 1 } : match.scores,
        },
      };
    }
  }
}
```

### lib/storage.ts

```tsx
import { DEFAULT_SETTINGS, EMPTY_STATS } from "@/lib/constants";
import type { GameSettings, GameStats, Player } from "@/types/game";

const object = (value: unknown): value is Record<string, unknown> => typeof value === "object" && value !== null && !Array.isArray(value);
const count = (value: unknown): value is number => typeof value === "number" && Number.isSafeInteger(value) && value >= 0;
const player = (value: unknown): value is Player => value === "X" || value === "O";

export function parseSettings(value: unknown): GameSettings {
  if (!object(value)) return DEFAULT_SETTINGS;
  const names = object(value.names) ? value.names : {};
  const name = (key: Player) => typeof names[key] === "string" && names[key].trim() ? names[key].trim().slice(0, 24) : DEFAULT_SETTINGS.names[key];
  return {
    names: { X: name("X"), O: name("O") },
    sound: typeof value.sound === "boolean" ? value.sound : true,
    haptics: typeof value.haptics === "boolean" ? value.haptics : true,
    theme: value.theme === "sunset" || value.theme === "ice" ? value.theme : "cosmic",
  };
}
export function parseStats(value: unknown): GameStats {
  if (!object(value) || !object(value.wins) || !count(value.totalRounds) || !count(value.draws) || !count(value.wins.X) || !count(value.wins.O)) return EMPTY_STATS;
  if (value.totalRounds !== value.wins.X + value.wins.O + value.draws) return EMPTY_STATS;
  const streak = (input: unknown): GameStats["streak"] => {
    if (!object(input) || !count(input.count) || input.count === 0 || !player(input.player)) return { player: null, count: 0 };
    const wins = value.wins;
    const playerWins = object(wins) ? wins[input.player] : undefined;
    if (!count(playerWins) || input.count > playerWins) return { player: null, count: 0 };
    return { player: input.player, count: input.count };
  };
  const current = streak(value.streak);
  const best = streak(value.bestStreak);
  return { totalRounds: value.totalRounds, wins: { X: value.wins.X, O: value.wins.O }, draws: value.draws, streak: current, bestStreak: best.count >= current.count ? best : current };
}
export function readStorage<T>(key: string, fallback: T, parse: (value: unknown) => T): { value: T; failed: boolean } {
  try {
    const raw = window.localStorage.getItem(key);
    if (raw === null) return { value: fallback, failed: false };
    try { return { value: parse(JSON.parse(raw) as unknown), failed: false }; }
    catch { return { value: fallback, failed: false }; }
  } catch { return { value: fallback, failed: true }; }
}
export function writeStorage<T>(key: string, value: T): boolean {
  try { window.localStorage.setItem(key, JSON.stringify(value)); return true; }
  catch { return false; }
}
```

### types/game.ts

```tsx
export type Player = "X" | "O";
export type CellValue = Player | null;
export type Board = [CellValue, CellValue, CellValue, CellValue, CellValue, CellValue, CellValue, CellValue, CellValue];
export type WinningLine = readonly [number, number, number];
export type GameStatus = "playing" | "won" | "draw";
export type Theme = "cosmic" | "sunset" | "ice";

export interface GameSettings {
  names: Record<Player, string>;
  sound: boolean;
  haptics: boolean;
  theme: Theme;
}

export interface GameStats {
  totalRounds: number;
  wins: Record<Player, number>;
  draws: number;
  streak: { player: Player | null; count: number };
  bestStreak: { player: Player | null; count: number };
}

export interface MatchState {
  board: Board;
  currentPlayer: Player;
  startingPlayer: Player;
  status: GameStatus;
  winner: Player | null;
  round: number;
  scores: Record<Player, number>;
}

export interface GameState {
  screen: "start" | "game";
  match: MatchState;
  stats: GameStats;
}

export type GameAction =
  | { type: "hydrate"; stats: GameStats }
  | { type: "start" }
  | { type: "leave" }
  | { type: "move"; index: number }
  | { type: "next" }
  | { type: "reset-match" }
  | { type: "reset-stats" };
```

### tests/gameLogic.test.ts

```tsx
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { EMPTY_STATS, WINNING_LINES } from "@/lib/constants";
import { checkWinner, createBoard, createGame, gameReducer, getNextStartingPlayer, getWinningLine, isBoardFull, recordResult } from "@/lib/gameLogic";
import type { GameState, Player } from "@/types/game";

const play = (moves: number[], state: GameState = gameReducer(createGame(), { type: "start" })) => moves.reduce((current, index) => gameReducer(current, { type: "move", index }), state);

describe("winning lines", () => {
  for (const player of ["X", "O"] as Player[]) {
    WINNING_LINES.forEach((line, index) => {
      it(`detects ${player} on line ${index + 1}`, () => {
        const board = createBoard();
        line.forEach(cell => { board[cell] = player; });
        expect(checkWinner(board)).toBe(player);
        expect(getWinningLine(board)).toEqual(line);
      });
    });
  }
  it("does not mark an empty board as won or full", () => {
    expect(checkWinner(createBoard())).toBeNull();
    expect(isBoardFull(createBoard())).toBe(false);
    expect(getWinningLine(createBoard())).toBeNull();
  });
});

describe("match transitions", () => {
  it("starts with X and rejects occupied and invalid cells", () => {
    const initial = gameReducer(createGame(), { type: "start" });
    expect(initial.match.currentPlayer).toBe("X");
    const state = play([0], initial);
    expect(state.match.currentPlayer).toBe("O");
    for (const index of [0, -1, 9, 1.5, NaN]) expect(gameReducer(state, { type: "move", index })).toBe(state);
  });
  it("records a win exactly once and locks the board", () => {
    const state = play([0, 3, 1, 4, 2]);
    expect(state.match.status).toBe("won");
    expect(state.match.scores).toEqual({ X: 1, O: 0 });
    expect(state.stats.totalRounds).toBe(1);
    const ignored = play([2, 6, 8], state);
    expect(ignored).toBe(state);
  });
  it("records an O win and lets X start next", () => {
    const state = play([0, 3, 1, 4, 8, 5]);
    expect(state.match.winner).toBe("O");
    expect(gameReducer(state, { type: "next" }).match.currentPlayer).toBe("X");
  });
  it("records a full-board draw and alternates the starter", () => {
    const state = play([0, 1, 2, 4, 3, 5, 7, 6, 8]);
    expect(state.match.status).toBe("draw");
    expect(state.stats.draws).toBe(1);
    expect(state.match.scores).toEqual({ X: 0, O: 0 });
    const next = gameReducer(state, { type: "next" });
    expect(next.match.currentPlayer).toBe("O");
    expect(next.match.round).toBe(2);
    expect(getNextStartingPlayer("O", null)).toBe("X");
  });
  it("lets the loser start and retains match scores", () => {
    const next = gameReducer(play([0, 3, 1, 4, 2]), { type: "next" });
    expect(next.match.startingPlayer).toBe("O");
    expect(next.match.scores.X).toBe(1);
    expect(next.match.board).toEqual(createBoard());
  });
  it("abandons an unfinished round without changing starter or totals", () => {
    const state = gameReducer(play([0, 3, 1, 4, 2]), { type: "next" });
    const restarted = gameReducer(play([4, 2], state), { type: "next" });
    expect(restarted.match.round).toBe(2);
    expect(restarted.match.currentPlayer).toBe("O");
    expect(restarted.stats.totalRounds).toBe(1);
  });
  it("resets or leaves a match without deleting lifetime statistics", () => {
    const state = play([0, 3, 1, 4, 2]);
    for (const type of ["reset-match", "leave"] as const) {
      const next = gameReducer(state, { type });
      expect(next.stats).toEqual(state.stats);
      expect(next.match.scores).toEqual({ X: 0, O: 0 });
      expect(next.match.round).toBe(1);
      expect(next.match.startingPlayer).toBe("X");
    }
  });
  it("clears stats separately and does not recount the finished match", () => {
    const state = gameReducer(play([0, 3, 1, 4, 2]), { type: "reset-stats" });
    expect(state.stats.totalRounds).toBe(0);
    expect(gameReducer(state, { type: "move", index: 8 }).stats.totalRounds).toBe(0);
  });
  it("tracks streaks across wins and breaks them on a draw or opponent win", () => {
    const twice = recordResult(recordResult(EMPTY_STATS, "X"), "X");
    expect(twice.streak).toEqual({ player: "X", count: 2 });
    const draw = recordResult(twice, null);
    expect(draw.streak).toEqual({ player: null, count: 0 });
    expect(draw.bestStreak).toEqual({ player: "X", count: 2 });
    expect(recordResult(twice, "O").streak).toEqual({ player: "O", count: 1 });
  });
});
```

### tests/storage.test.ts

```tsx
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { DEFAULT_SETTINGS, EMPTY_STATS } from "@/lib/constants";
import { parseSettings, parseStats, readStorage, writeStorage } from "@/lib/storage";

afterEach(() => vi.unstubAllGlobals());
describe("storage validation", () => {
  it("recovers from malformed or missing settings and bounds names", () => {
    expect(parseSettings(null)).toEqual(DEFAULT_SETTINGS);
    const settings = parseSettings({ names: { X: " ", O: "أ".repeat(50) }, sound: false, haptics: "true", theme: "invalid" });
    expect(settings.names.X).toBe(DEFAULT_SETTINGS.names.X);
    expect(settings.names.O).toHaveLength(24);
    expect(settings.sound).toBe(false);
    expect(settings.haptics).toBe(true);
    expect(settings.theme).toBe("cosmic");
  });
  it("rejects inconsistent or invalid totals", () => {
    for (const value of [null, [], { ...EMPTY_STATS, totalRounds: -1 }, { ...EMPTY_STATS, totalRounds: 5 }, { ...EMPTY_STATS, draws: 1.5 }]) expect(parseStats(value)).toEqual(EMPTY_STATS);
  });
  it("repairs impossible streak values", () => {
    expect(parseStats({ ...EMPTY_STATS, streak: { player: "X", count: 100 } }).streak.count).toBe(0);
  });
  it("handles malformed JSON without crashing", () => {
    vi.stubGlobal("window", { localStorage: { getItem: () => "{broken" } });
    expect(readStorage("settings", DEFAULT_SETTINGS, parseSettings)).toEqual({ value: DEFAULT_SETTINGS, failed: false });
  });
  it("handles denied reads and writes", () => {
    vi.stubGlobal("window", { localStorage: { getItem: () => { throw new Error("Denied"); }, setItem: () => { throw new Error("Quota"); } } });
    expect(readStorage("stats", EMPTY_STATS, parseStats)).toEqual({ value: EMPTY_STATS, failed: true });
    expect(writeStorage("stats", EMPTY_STATS)).toBe(false);
  });
});
```

### tests/e2e/game.spec.ts

```tsx
import { expect, test, type Page } from "@playwright/test";

const start = async (page: Page) => { await page.goto("/"); await page.getByRole("button", { name: "ابدأ الجولة", exact: true }).click(); };
const moves = async (page: Page, indexes: number[]) => { for (const index of indexes) await page.locator(`[data-cell="${index}"]`).click(); };

test("win, lock, next starter, and persistent lifetime stats", async ({ page }) => {
  await start(page);
  await moves(page, [0, 3, 1, 4, 2]);
  const result = page.getByRole("dialog", { name: "انتصار ساحق!" });
  await expect(result).toBeVisible();
  await result.getByRole("button", { name: "إغلاق", exact: true }).click();
  await expect(page.locator('[data-cell="8"]')).toHaveAttribute("aria-disabled", "true");
  await page.locator('[data-cell="8"]').click({ force: true });
  await expect(page.locator('[data-cell="8"] svg')).toHaveCount(0);
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "جولة جديدة", exact: true }).click();
  await expect(page.getByRole("status")).toContainText("دور اللاعب ○ الآن");
  await page.reload();
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "الإحصاءات", exact: true }).click();
  await expect(page.locator(".total-rounds strong")).toHaveText("1");
  await expect(page.locator(".stat-trio .text-x").last()).toHaveText("1");
});

test("draw and next round alternate starter", async ({ page }) => {
  await start(page);
  await moves(page, [0, 1, 2, 4, 3, 5, 7, 6, 8]);
  const dialog = page.getByRole("dialog", { name: "تعادل ذكي" });
  await expect(dialog).toBeVisible();
  await dialog.getByRole("button", { name: "الجولة التالية" }).click();
  await expect(page.getByRole("status")).toContainText("دور اللاعب ○ الآن");
});

test("nested reset confirmation releases scroll and does not erase statistics", async ({ page }) => {
  await start(page);
  await moves(page, [0, 3, 1, 4, 2]);
  await page.getByRole("dialog", { name: "انتصار ساحق!" }).getByRole("button", { name: "إعادة المباراة" }).click();
  await page.getByRole("dialog", { name: "نبدأ مباراة جديدة؟" }).getByRole("button", { name: "إعادة المباراة" }).click();
  await expect(page.getByRole("dialog")).toHaveCount(0);
  expect(await page.evaluate(() => document.body.style.overflow)).not.toBe("hidden");
  await expect(page.locator(".game-cell svg")).toHaveCount(0);
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "العودة للرئيسية" }).click();
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "الإحصاءات", exact: true }).click();
  await expect(page.locator(".total-rounds strong")).toHaveText("1");
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "تصفير الإحصاءات", exact: true }).click();
  const confirm = page.getByRole("dialog", { name: "تصفير سجل الساحة؟" });
  await confirm.getByRole("button", { name: "تراجع" }).click();
  await expect(page.locator(".total-rounds strong")).toHaveText("1");
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "تصفير الإحصاءات", exact: true }).click();
  await confirm.getByRole("button", { name: "تصفير الإحصاءات", exact: true }).click();
  await expect(page.getByText("الساحة تنتظر أول انتصار")).toBeVisible();
});

test("settings update instantly, persist, and reset only after confirmation", async ({ page }) => {
  await page.goto("/");
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "الإعدادات", exact: true }).click();
  await page.getByRole("textbox", { name: "اسم اللاعب ×" }).fill("سارة");
  await page.getByLabel("غروب بنفسجي", { exact: true }).check();
  await expect(page.locator(".app-shell")).toHaveAttribute("data-theme", "sunset");
  await page.getByRole("switch", { name: "المؤثرات الصوتية" }).click();
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "إغلاق", exact: true }).click();
  await page.reload();
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "الإعدادات", exact: true }).click();
  await expect(page.getByRole("textbox", { name: "اسم اللاعب ×" })).toHaveValue("سارة");
  await expect(page.getByRole("switch", { name: "المؤثرات الصوتية" })).toHaveAttribute("aria-checked", "false");
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "إعادة الإعدادات الافتراضية" }).click();
  const confirmation = page.getByRole("dialog", { name: "استعادة الإعدادات؟" });
  await confirmation.getByRole("button", { name: "تراجع" }).click();
  await expect(page.getByRole("textbox", { name: "اسم اللاعب ×" })).toHaveValue("سارة");
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "إعادة الإعدادات الافتراضية" }).click();
  await confirmation.getByRole("button", { name: "استعادة الافتراضي" }).click();
  await expect(page.getByRole("textbox", { name: "اسم اللاعب ×" })).toHaveValue("اللاعب ×");
  await expect(page.locator(".app-shell")).toHaveAttribute("data-theme", "cosmic");
});

test("cancel unfinished round and confirm leaving", async ({ page }) => {
  await start(page);
  await moves(page, [0, 1]);
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "جولة جديدة", exact: true }).click();
  await page.getByRole("dialog").getByRole("button", { name: "جولة جديدة", exact: true }).click();
  await expect(page.locator(".game-cell svg")).toHaveCount(0);
  await expect(page.getByRole("status")).toContainText("دور اللاعب × الآن");
  await moves(page, [4]);
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "العودة للرئيسية" }).click();
  const confirmation = page.getByRole("dialog", { name: "تغادران الساحة؟" });
  await confirmation.getByRole("button", { name: "تراجع" }).click();
  await expect(page.locator(".game-cell svg")).toHaveCount(1);
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "العودة للرئيسية" }).click();
  await confirmation.getByRole("button", { name: "العودة للرئيسية" }).click();
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "ابدأ الجولة", exact: true }).click();
  await expect(page.locator(".game-cell svg")).toHaveCount(0);
});

test("corrupted storage, RTL, reduced motion and viewport fit", async ({ page }) => {
  await page.addInitScript(() => {
    localStorage.setItem("nuqta-daira:stats:v1", "bad json");
    localStorage.setItem("nuqta-daira:settings:v1", JSON.stringify({ theme: "unknown", names: null }));
  });
  await page.emulateMedia({ reducedMotion: "reduce" });
  await start(page);
  await expect(page.locator("html")).toHaveAttribute("dir", "rtl");
  await expect(page.locator("html")).toHaveAttribute("lang", "ar");
  expect(await page.evaluate(() => document.documentElement.scrollWidth <= innerWidth)).toBe(true);
  const bounds = await page.locator(".game-controls").boundingBox();
  expect(bounds).not.toBeNull();
  expect(bounds!.y + bounds!.height).toBeLessThanOrEqual(page.viewportSize()!.height);
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "الإعدادات", exact: true }).click();
  await page.keyboard.press("Escape");
  await expect(page.getByRole("dialog")).toHaveCount(0);
  await expect(page.getByRole("button", { name: "الإعدادات", exact: true })).toBeFocused();
});
```

## Final Verification

- شغّل المشروع محليًا عبر `npm install` ثم `npm run dev` وافتح `http://localhost:3000`.
- لاختبار فوز X يدويًا: اضغط الخانات 1،4،2،5،3. يجب أن يظهر خط الفوز ومودال الانتصار، ثم تبدأ الجولة التالية باللاعب الخاسر ○.
- لاختبار التعادل: اضغط 1،2،3،5،4،6،8،7،9. يجب أن يظهر مودال "تعادل ذكي" وتبدأ الجولة التالية باللاعب الذي لم يبدأ الجولة السابقة.
- لاختبار LocalStorage: غيّر الأسماء والثيم والصوت من الإعدادات، العب جولة مكتملة، حدّث الصفحة وتحقق من بقاء الإعدادات والإحصاءات.
- لاختبار RTL والموبايل: افحص الصفحة بعرض 360px وتأكد أن `html` يحمل `dir="rtl"` و`lang="ar"` وأن لوحة اللعب وأزرار التحكم داخل ارتفاع الشاشة.
- تم التحقق آليًا بالأوامر: `npm run lint`، `npm run typecheck`، `npm test`، `npm run build`، و`npm run test:e2e`.
